2026-11-01T06:22:34.3325156Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Repeat PP4 round 14 on its pinned image with the prepack gate matching GPUs by UUID

Round 14 (41264009819) scored 10009.54 with P: on pair calls the old
FP32 writer, a 128-bit V prepack and native-only FA3. Its margin over
10,000 is +0.1 percent, below the 0.15 percent run-to-run spread, so it
needs a repeat. Round 15 (41273087893) should have been one: P passed
the gate again on all 12 H200s with the same gain (33.0-38.7 us per call
at 3994+3994), but its aggregate refused with "wrong GPU" and serving
fell back to round 12 (9947.81).

Cause: the aggregate matched each prepack fragment to the pair gate's
result for the same worker and rank. On g1, prefill-0 and prefill-1
start each gate with concurrent srun calls and get GPUs 0-3 or 4-7 in
whichever order the calls land, so the two sets can swap between gates.
They did in round 15; the twelve UUIDs were the same. Round 14 happened
to keep the order.

Fix (by Codex, first committed as ebe7b14 on
perf/pp4-r16-gatefix-20261101, and carried here unchanged so that the
push starts serving):
- Index the adopted pair gate by its twelve unique UUIDs. Every prepack
  fragment must name an unused UUID from that set, report an H200 and
  match that UUID's allocation prefix; the fragment's worker and rank
  only have to agree with its own path. The two UUID sets must be equal.
- Everything else is unchanged: schema, run ID, image and binary
  hashes, health, deadlines, numeric and timing checks, P before S,
  atomic env publication.
- A compressed fixture keeps round 15's pair selection and all twelve
  prepack fragments; tests replay the real swap and cover foreign,
  duplicate and missing UUIDs, the wrong allocation and non-H200
  devices. The other gate aggregates on main do not map GPUs through
  worker and rank across gates.
- The round-14 research note records the cause and the fix.

This is main's round-14 configuration otherwise: same config, kernels,
sbatch and gate group (diagnostics, P, then S), no build. The Firmus
selection reuses the pinned image 5a3df8c4 from 41264009819.

Validation: the full pytest suite exits 0 on this tree; Codex reported
1387 passed and 78 skipped.
